# StackPick: 1,000-idea software content and affiliate roadmap

**Prepared:** 2026-10-17  
**Scope:** US-focused small-business and freelancer software discovery, comparison, pricing, alternatives, implementation and affiliate-ready content.  
**Status:** Research backlog, not a publication queue. These are candidate topics; they are not claims of search volume, ranking difficulty, affiliate approval or expected revenue.

## Operating rule

Do **not** publish all 1,000 pages simply because they are listed. This is a long-term editorial inventory for finding useful, distinct topics around products StackPick may apply to promote later. Before any topic becomes a URL, check Google Search Console queries and existing page intent, verify demand with current research, confirm a distinct reader problem, and decide whether it should be a new page, a section on an existing page, or not published. Merge overlapping intent rather than generating doorway pages.

## Priority tiers

- **P0 candidate (ideas 1–150):** start with current monetization-aligned product clusters, especially HubSpot CRM, FreshBooks, Zoho CRM/Invoice, Jotform, Systeme.io, MailerLite, Brevo, monday.com, ClickUp, Webflow, Kinsta, and other categories already relevant to StackPick. This is a research priority, not an assertion of affiliate eligibility.
- **P1 candidate (ideas 151–400):** products and workflows with a plausible commercial intent, once P0 pages have official source links and editorial quality checks.
- **P2 research backlog (ideas 401–1000):** publish only where Search Console, SERP review, audience fit and content differentiation support the page.

Priority in the table is assigned by backlog order only. It does not represent measured search demand.

## Required research and publishing gates

1. **Search intent:** inspect the US results page and current GSC queries. Record the query, date, search intent, competing page types, and why StackPick can add something useful. Do not invent volume or difficulty.
2. **Existing URL overlap:** search the sitemap and repository for similar titles/slugs. Prefer improving an existing URL if it already satisfies the intent.
3. **Official fact-checking:** use vendor pricing pages, help docs, plan-limit pages, terms and regional availability pages. Record checked date, currency, billing basis, promotional caveats, per-seat vs account pricing, usage caps and renewal pricing where published.
4. **Evidence standard:** call desk research desk research. Claim hands-on testing only after real testing is recorded. Do not fabricate screenshots, benchmarks, customer experience, test scores or personal use.
5. **Reader value:** include a clear audience, fit/not-fit, meaningful trade-offs, switching costs, data export, cancellation, integrations and relevant alternatives. Avoid filler word counts.
6. **Affiliate eligibility:** before applying or adding affiliate links, verify official program terms, Sri Lankan publisher eligibility, payout method/minimum, attribution window, commission terms, brand bidding restrictions, disclosure requirements and traffic thresholds. Record the official source and date. Current program status is **unverified unless the owner checks the partner dashboard**.
7. **Monetization disclosure:** show a clear affiliate disclosure near commercial links after a program is approved and links are active. Keep useful non-affiliate choices and editorial independence.
8. **Quality release:** check title/meta/canonical, internal links, mobile rendering, source links, update date, accessibility, affiliate link attributes and build/lint/link-check results.
9. **Measurement:** track GSC clicks/impressions/queries by URL, GA4 engaged sessions, outbound affiliate clicks, approved referrals and confirmed earnings separately. Do not treat CPC or third-party traffic estimates as publisher revenue.
10. **Release pace:** begin with a small group of thoroughly researched pages; expand only after they have been crawled and measured. The list is not a promise to publish 1,000 URLs.

## Recommended page types

- **Product profiles:** who it suits, plan facts, strengths, limitations, official links and alternatives.
- **Comparisons:** clear decision criteria, dated fact matrix, use-case verdicts and meaningful trade-offs.
- **Pricing/limits:** billing basis, free tier vs trial, seats/contacts/usage caps, add-ons and renewal caveats.
- **Alternatives:** reasons to switch, migration considerations and when the original product remains preferable.
- **Use-case guides:** workflow-specific advice, templates/checklists and realistic setup steps.
- **Finder/tools:** interactive navigation that helps readers narrow choices without presenting unverified pricing or paid placement as objective ranking.
- **Migration/integration:** vendor-supported steps, export/import constraints, common failure points and rollback considerations.

## Product and program status

The existence of a topic in this plan does not mean the product has an affiliate program, that StackPick is eligible, or that an application has been submitted. Track each partner separately in `docs/stackpick-affiliate-tracker.md`. Prioritize investigation of HubSpot, FreshBooks, Jotform, Systeme.io, monday.com, Webflow and Kinsta based on the project context; verify current official terms and Sri Lankan payout support before acting. Do not prioritize Pipedrive in this plan.

## How to turn this backlog into an execution board

For each chosen idea, add these fields to the working tracker before writing:
- **Existing URL / decision:** refresh, add a section, create new URL, merge, or reject.
- **Target query and intent:** supported by GSC, SERP inspection or a documented keyword research source.
- **Affiliate partner:** program name, official terms URL, application status, eligibility, payout method, and checked date.
- **Evidence sources:** primary sources and exact claims each source supports.
- **Outline:** original reader problem, differentiating angle, comparison criteria, limitations and internal links.
- **Status:** unresearched → validated → brief approved → draft → fact-check → QA → published → measured.
- **Review date:** especially for prices, free-tier limits and partner terms.

## First 30-day execution order

1. Launch and QA the **Software Finder** and link it from primary navigation; use it to route users to relevant existing guides rather than creating 20 thin category pages.
2. Add the **How We Verify** editorial standards page and link it from the finder and footer.
3. Improve current product profiles for the affiliate candidates that already have related content, using dated official facts and genuine trade-offs.
4. Choose only 5–8 pages from P0 after a URL-overlap audit. Suggested first candidates: FreshBooks pricing/limits, Jotform pricing/alternatives, Systeme.io vs a verified alternative, monday.com pricing/limits, Webflow vs a verified alternative, Kinsta vs a verified alternative, HubSpot CRM free limits, and Zoho Invoice vs a verified alternative.
5. Verify each partner's official application rules and payout eligibility separately. Do not publish affiliate claims or add tracking links until approved.
6. Capture a GSC and GA4 baseline before release, then review 28-day and 90-day performance. Keep low-impression results labeled as inconclusive.

## Guardrails

- No fixed minimum word count; use enough detail to solve the reader's task.
- No unsupported keyword volumes, KD scores, conversion rates, RPMs or income promises.
- No mass-produced near-duplicate pages or invented first-hand testing.
- No fake review/rating structured data.
- No affiliate applications, link activations or deployments are implied by this content plan.
